shared_focus = {
	id = GRE_Clean_Up_the_Ranks
	icon = GFX_WAL_Anti-Unionist_Purge
	cost = 3
	prerequisite = {
		focus = GRE_Rebrand_the_Old
	}
	relative_position_id = GRE_Rebrand_the_Old
	x = -1
	y = 1
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus GRE_Clean_Up_the_Ranks"
		add_stability = 0.10
		hidden_effect = {	
			for_each_loop = { #Uses index as state modified var
				array = GRE_Resistance_Government_Control
				if = {
					limit = { 
						NOT = { # Exclude Athens
							check_variable = { i = 2 } 
						} 
					}
					set_temp_variable = {
						GRE_Resistance_State_Mod = i
					}
					set_temp_variable = {
						GRE_Resistance_Amount = 5
					}
					set_temp_variable = {
						GRE_Resistance_Array = 1
					}
					GRE_Resistance_Modify_Control = yes
				}
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = GRE_CleanRanksStability
	}
}
